    quote: "{\n  \"page_type\": \"text\",\n  \"pdf_page\": 6,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n\
      \  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial\
      \ Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026\
      \ \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill of Quantities (BOQ)\
      \ & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the entire Tender and Contract\
      \ Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract (including ‘Contract Data’),\
      \ \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications, \\nEmployer’s Requirements,\
      \ Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities\
      \ given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional). The basis of payment\
      \ will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried out, as measured by the Contractor and verified\
      \ by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”.\
      \ \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause\
      \ 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to\
      \ select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A,\
      \ for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer\
      \ shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount\
      \ indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of\
      \ any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased\
      \ according to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor\
      \ should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price\
      \ and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable\
      \ taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month\
      \ previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The method\
      \ of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in\
      \ the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the\
      \ tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all\
      \ respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional\
      \ plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris\
      \ at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts,\
      \ all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection\
      \ facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/\
      \ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining\
      \ land & building while working. \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead\
      \ charges, \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities\
      \ \\nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the\
      \ Defect Liability Period.  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff\
      \ & workmen, \\nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\\
      ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        14.760004043579102,\n        37.509437561035156,\n        519.9669799804688,\n\
      \        56.52032470703125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \                                        Financial Document \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\

    json_path: parsed_json/vol4/pricing_document.json
    json_sha256: e818be1fb28ea74092cb53a451b92e169c6e8a07afec0add897be67d5844a2b3

    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"page_type\": \"text\",\n  \"pdf_page\": 6,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n\
      \  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial\
      \ Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026\
      \ \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill of Quantities (BOQ)\
      \ & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the entire Tender and Contract\
      \ Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract (including ‘Contract Data’),\
      \ \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications, \\nEmployer’s Requirements,\
      \ Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities\
      \ given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional). The basis of payment\
      \ will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried out, as measured by the Contractor and verified\
      \ by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”.\
      \ \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause\
      \ 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to\
      \ select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A,\
      \ for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer\
      \ shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount\
      \ indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of\
      \ any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased\
      \ according to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor\
      \ should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price\
      \ and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable\
      \ taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month\
      \ previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The method\
      \ of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in\
      \ the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the\
      \ tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all\
      \ respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional\
      \ plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris\
      \ at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts,\
      \ all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection\
      \ facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/\
      \ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining\
      \ land & building while working. \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead\
      \ charges, \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities\
      \ \\nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the\
      \ Defect Liability Period.  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff\
      \ & workmen, \\nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\\
      ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        14.760004043579102,\n        37.509437561035156,\n        519.9669799804688,\n\
      \        56.52032470703125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
      \                                        Financial Document \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n\
      \        71.99996948242188,\n        62.5140495300293,\n        74.49351501464844,\n        69.48028564453125\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n      \
      \  71.99996948242188,\n        777.8273315429688,\n        74.38514709472656,\n        786.9194946289062\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n      \
      \  71.99996948242188,\n        802.54736328125,\n        525.8132934570312,\n        823.079833984375\n  \
      \    ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.             \
      \               September 2026 \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n      \
      \  227.51991271972656,\n        74.64318084716797,\n        377.9470520019531,\n        84.99869537353516\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"Pricing Document-Preamble \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        99.36321258544922,\n        526.3881225585938,\n \
      \       177.9987030029297\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"1. The Pricing Document-Bill\
      \ of Quantities (BOQ) & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the entire\
      \ Tender and Contract Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract (including\
      \ ‘Contract Data’), \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications, \\\
      nEmployer’s Requirements, Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum and Addendums.\
      \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.999267578125,\n        187.3231201171875,\n        526.3868408203125,\n\
      \        238.59861755371094\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"2. The quantities given\
      \ in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional). The basis of payment will be\
      \ the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried out, as measured by the Contractor and verified by\
      \ the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”.\
      \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99929809570312,\n        247.923095703125,\n       \
      \ 526.387451171875,\n        326.4385681152344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 7,\n      \"text\": \"3.\
      \ Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause 10.\
      \ Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to select\
      \ option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A,\
      \ for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer\
      \ shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n   \
      \   \"bbox\": [\n        71.99847412109375,\n        335.7630310058594,\n        526.3863525390625,\n    \
      \    387.0384826660156\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"4. The Lump sum amount indicated\
      \ for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of any variation\
      \ in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according\
      \ to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n     \
      \ \"bbox\": [\n        71.99948120117188,\n        396.36297607421875,\n        526.3870239257812,\n     \
      \   420.3984680175781\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"5. The Contractor should note\
      \ that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price and not on the\
      \ estimate cost put to tender. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99951171875,\n        429.72296142578125,\n\
      \        526.3870239257812,\n        467.31842041015625\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 10,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand\
      \ statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month previous to the \\nmonth of invitation\
      \ of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00054931640625,\n\
      \        476.6429138183594,\n        526.3883666992188,\n        514.2384033203125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 11,\n      \"text\": \"7. The method of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance\
      \ with \\nthe requirements as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument-\
      \ Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n     \
      \   72.00054931640625,\n        523.5628662109375,\n        526.3883666992188,\n        602.1983642578125\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"8. The quoted price is for completed and finished\
      \ items of work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be\
      \ deemed to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power,\
      \ water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished\
      \ material), all leads & lifts, all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance,\
      \ night working and inspection facilities. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00051879882812,\n\
      \        611.5228271484375,\n        526.3880615234375,\n        649.1182861328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work\
      \ sites/ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection\
      \ of adjoining land & building while working. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00051879882812,\n\
      \        658.4427490234375,\n        526.3875732421875,\n        709.71826171875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 14,\n      \"text\": \"10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead charges,\
      \ \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities \\nand obligations\
      \ set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the Defect Liability Period.\
      \  \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99981689453125,\n        719.042724609375,\n      \
      \  526.3865966796875,\n        756.6382446289062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"\
      11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff & workmen, \\nall insurance\
      \ costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\ncharges payable to various\
      \ departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n\
      \        24.479991912841797,\n        570.0199584960938,\n        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 16,\n      \"text\": \"305\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified

  - id: S19382a03427e15ba
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Financial/vol4_secJ.md
    okf_sha256: 9439a74058830fd65e3d20892e276927f56b1c5717d424ea357f19aca5698795
    heading: 1.0 — Pricing Document Preamble and Instructions
    source_document: Vol4DLNP1.pdf
    pdf_pages:
    - 6
    quote: "- **Read BOQ with the Complete Tender and Contract Documents** (Instruction 1)\n- **Provisional Quantities\
      \ and Payment for Measured Work** (Instruction 2)\n- **Price Bid Preparation in the Prescribed XLS Sheet**\
      \ (Instruction 3; ITT 10)\n  - Schedule-A: percentage rate to two decimals; At Par / Excess / Less\n  - Schedule-B:\
      \ At Par only\n  - Schedule-C: lump-sum salvage credit to BMRCL\n- **Proportionate Recovery and Adjustment\
      \ of Salvage Credit** (Instruction 4)\n- **Salvage Credit Based on Quoted Price** (Instruction 5)\n- **Taxes\
      \ and Statutory Levies Included, Except GST** (Instruction 6)\n- **Measurement of Completed Work** (Instruction\
      \ 7)\n- **Completed-Work Pricing and Included Execution Costs** (Instruction 8)\n- **Included Safety and Protection\
      \ Costs** (Instruction 9)\n- **Overheads, Profit, Risks and Defect-Remedy Obligations** (Instruction 10)\n\
      - **Accommodation, Insurance, Wages and Approval Costs** (Instruction 11)\n\n\n\n- **Execution Standards and\
      \ Engineer’s Directions** (Instruction 12)\n- **Incidental Removal Works Without Separate Payment** (Instruction\
      \ 13)\n- **Temporary Relocation and Protection of Utilities** (Instruction 14)\n- **Temporary Works, Power\
      \ and Supports at Contractor’s Cost** (Instruction 15)\n- **Permanent Supports and Restoration Payment Provisions**\
      \ (Instruction 16)\n- **Balcony and Canopy Area at 50%; Chejjas Excluded from Payment** (Instruction 17)\n\
      - **Demolition Depth and Refilling of Low-Level Areas** (Instruction 18)\n- **Removal Within the Line of Cut,\
      \ Excluding Trees** (Instruction 19)\n- **Total Floor Area as the Sum of Built Areas of All Floors** (Instruction\
      \ 20)\n- **Building Additions or Deletions and Accepted Percentage Rates** (Instruction 21)"
    page_basis: OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified
    page_precision: section_reference
  - id: S469ca02217533c37
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Financial/vol4_secJ.md
    okf_sha256: 9439a74058830fd65e3d20892e276927f56b1c5717d424ea357f19aca5698795
    heading: 2.0 — Abstract of Bill of Quantities (BOQ)
    source_document: Vol4DLNP1.pdf
    pdf_pages:
    - 8
    quote: "- **Schedule-A — Demolition and Dismantling**\n  - Partial & Full demolition and dismantling work as\
      \ per details given in Schedule-A of the BOQ\n  - **Estimated Cost:** INR 4,69,41,306.00\n\n- **Schedule-B\
      \ — KPWD Schedule-of-Rate Items**\n  - Items under Schedule of Rate published by KPWD\n  - **Estimated Cost:**\
      \ INR 5,00,000.00\n\n- **Total of Schedules A+B:** INR 4,74,41,306.00\n\n- **Schedule-C — Salvage Credit to\
      \ BMRCL**\n  - Credit for salvageable items from dismantled/demolished private properties in which the owner\
      \ has no interest\n  - **Source Reference:** Para 15 of Part-3 of Employer’s Requirement\n  - **Estimated\
      \ Cost Field:** `----`\n  - **Pricing Basis:** Lump-sum credit under Preamble Instruction 3"
    page_basis: OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified
    page_precision: section_reference

        quote: "- **Read BOQ with the Complete Tender and Contract Documents** (Instruction 1)\n- **Provisional\
          \ Quantities and Payment for Measured Work** (Instruction 2)\n- **Price Bid Preparation in the Prescribed\
          \ XLS Sheet** (Instruction 3; ITT 10)\n  - Schedule-A: percentage rate to two decimals; At Par / Excess\
          \ / Less\n  - Schedule-B: At Par only\n  - Schedule-C: lump-sum salvage credit to BMRCL\n- **Proportionate\
          \ Recovery and Adjustment of Salvage Credit** (Instruction 4)\n- **Salvage Credit Based on Quoted Price**\
          \ (Instruction 5)\n- **Taxes and Statutory Levies Included, Except GST** (Instruction 6)\n- **Measurement\
          \ of Completed Work** (Instruction 7)\n- **Completed-Work Pricing and Included Execution Costs** (Instruction\
          \ 8)\n- **Included Safety and Protection Costs** (Instruction 9)\n- **Overheads, Profit, Risks and Defect-Remedy\
          \ Obligations** (Instruction 10)\n- **Accommodation, Insurance, Wages and Approval Costs** (Instruction\
          \ 11)\n\n\n\n- **Execution Standards and Engineer’s Directions** (Instruction 12)\n- **Incidental Removal\
          \ Works Without Separate Payment** (Instruction 13)\n- **Temporary Relocation and Protection of Utilities**\
          \ (Instruction 14)\n- **Temporary Works, Power and Supports at Contractor’s Cost** (Instruction 15)\n\
          - **Permanent Supports and Restoration Payment Provisions** (Instruction 16)\n- **Balcony and Canopy Area\
          \ at 50%; Chejjas Excluded from Payment** (Instruction 17)\n- **Demolition Depth and Refilling of Low-Level\
          \ Areas** (Instruction 18)\n- **Removal Within the Line of Cut, Excluding Trees** (Instruction 19)\n-\
          \ **Total Floor Area as the Sum of Built Areas of All Floors** (Instruction 20)\n- **Building Additions\
          \ or Deletions and Accepted Percentage Rates** (Instruction 21)"
        page_basis: OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified
        page_precision: section_reference

        quote: "{\n  \"page_type\": \"text\",\n  \"pdf_page\": 6,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
          ,\n  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                   \
          \     Financial Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                        \
          \    September 2026 \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill\
          \ of Quantities (BOQ) & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the\
          \ entire Tender and Contract Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract\
          \ (including ‘Contract Data’), \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications,\
          \ \\nEmployer’s Requirements, Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum\
          \ and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated\
          \ \\n(provisional). The basis of payment will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried\
          \ out, as measured by the Contractor and verified by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted\
          \ priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”. \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls\
          \ format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage\
          \ rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter\
          \ percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A, for Schedule-B tenderer is required to\
          \ \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL\
          \ on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount indicated for Schedule C will\
          \ be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of any variation in Quantities\
          \ (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according to the value\
          \ of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor should note that\
          \ the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price and not on the\
          \ estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes,\
          \ duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month\
          \ previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The\
          \ method of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements\
          \ as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities\
          \ or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of\
          \ work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed\
          \ to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power,\
          \ water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility\
          \ (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts, all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing,\
          \ watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will\
          \ be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for\
          \ workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining land & building while working.\
          \ \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead charges, \\ncontractor’s\
          \ profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities \\nand obligations set\
          \ out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the Defect Liability Period.\
          \  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff & workmen, \\nall\
          \ insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\ncharges payable\
          \ to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n\
          \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        14.760004043579102,\n        37.509437561035156,\n        519.9669799804688,\n\
          \        56.52032470703125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
          \                                        Financial Document \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\":\
          \ [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        62.5140495300293,\n        74.49351501464844,\n        69.48028564453125\n\
          \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n  \
          \      71.99996948242188,\n        777.8273315429688,\n        74.38514709472656,\n        786.9194946289062\n\

        quote: "{\n  \"page_type\": \"text\",\n  \"pdf_page\": 6,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
          ,\n  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                   \
          \     Financial Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                        \
          \    September 2026 \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill\
          \ of Quantities (BOQ) & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the\
          \ entire Tender and Contract Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract\
          \ (including ‘Contract Data’), \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications,\
          \ \\nEmployer’s Requirements, Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum\
          \ and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated\
          \ \\n(provisional). The basis of payment will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried\
          \ out, as measured by the Contractor and verified by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted\
          \ priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”. \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls\
          \ format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage\
          \ rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter\
          \ percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A, for Schedule-B tenderer is required to\
          \ \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL\
          \ on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount indicated for Schedule C will\
          \ be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of any variation in Quantities\
          \ (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according to the value\
          \ of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor should note that\
          \ the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price and not on the\
          \ estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes,\
          \ duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month\
          \ previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The\
          \ method of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements\
          \ as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities\
          \ or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of\
          \ work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed\
          \ to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power,\
          \ water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility\
          \ (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts, all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing,\
          \ watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will\
          \ be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for\
          \ workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining land & building while working.\
          \ \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead charges, \\ncontractor’s\
          \ profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities \\nand obligations set\
          \ out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the Defect Liability Period.\
          \  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff & workmen, \\nall\
          \ insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\ncharges payable\
          \ to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n\
          \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        14.760004043579102,\n        37.509437561035156,\n        519.9669799804688,\n\
          \        56.52032470703125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
          \                                        Financial Document \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\":\
          \ [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        62.5140495300293,\n        74.49351501464844,\n        69.48028564453125\n\
          \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n  \
          \      71.99996948242188,\n        777.8273315429688,\n        74.38514709472656,\n        786.9194946289062\n\
          \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n  \
          \      71.99996948242188,\n        802.54736328125,\n        525.8132934570312,\n        823.079833984375\n\
          \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.       \
          \                     September 2026 \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\":\
          \ [\n        227.51991271972656,\n        74.64318084716797,\n        377.9470520019531,\n        84.99869537353516\n\

          \ of Quantities (BOQ) & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the\
          \ entire Tender and Contract Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract\
          \ (including ‘Contract Data’), \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications,\
          \ \\nEmployer’s Requirements, Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum\
          \ and Addendums. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.999267578125,\n        187.3231201171875,\n\

          text\": \"2. The quantities given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional).\

          text\": \"4. The Lump sum amount indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount\
          \ bills proportionately. In case of any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit\
          \ to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills\
          \ on pro-rata basis. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99948120117188,\n        396.36297607421875,\n\
          \        526.3870239257812,\n        420.3984680175781\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"\
          text\": \"5. The Contractor should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\\
          nreference to the quoted price and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n    \
          \  \"bbox\": [\n        71.99951171875,\n        429.72296142578125,\n        526.3870239257812,\n   \
          \     467.31842041015625\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 10,\n      \"text\": \"6. The ‘Tender Price’\
          \ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as\
          \ per the laws in force as on last day of the month previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender,\
          \ except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00054931640625,\n\
          \        476.6429138183594,\n        526.3883666992188,\n        514.2384033203125\n      ],\n      \"\
          block_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"7. The method of measurement of completed work for payment shall\
          \ be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in\
          \ the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n\
          \      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00054931640625,\n        523.5628662109375,\n        526.3883666992188,\n\
          \        602.1983642578125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"8. The quoted price\
          \ is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions\
          \ of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel,\
          \ oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified\
          \ C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts, all temporary\

    quote: "{\n  \"page_type\": \"text\",\n  \"pdf_page\": 6,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n\
      \  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial\
      \ Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026\
      \ \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill of Quantities (BOQ)\
      \ & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the entire Tender and Contract\
      \ Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract (including ‘Contract Data’),\
      \ \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications, \\nEmployer’s Requirements,\
      \ Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities\
      \ given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional). The basis of payment\
      \ will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried out, as measured by the Contractor and verified\
      \ by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”.\
      \ \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause\
      \ 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to\
      \ select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A,\
      \ for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer\
      \ shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount\
      \ indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of\
      \ any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased\
      \ according to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor\
      \ should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price\
      \ and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable\
      \ taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month\
      \ previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The method\
      \ of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in\
      \ the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the\
      \ tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all\
      \ respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional\
      \ plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris\
      \ at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts,\
      \ all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection\
      \ facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/\
      \ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining\
      \ land & building while working. \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead\
      \ charges, \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities\
      \ \\nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the\
      \ Defect Liability Period.  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff\
      \ & workmen, \\nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\\
      ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\
      : [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        14.760004043579102,\n        37.509437561035156,\n        519.9669799804688,\n\
      \        56.52032470703125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\

  - id: S19382a03427e15ba
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Financial/vol4_secJ.md
    okf_sha256: 9439a74058830fd65e3d20892e276927f56b1c5717d424ea357f19aca5698795
    heading: 1.0 — Pricing Document Preamble and Instructions
    source_document: Vol4DLNP1.pdf
    pdf_pages:
    - 6
    quote: "- **Read BOQ with the Complete Tender and Contract Documents** (Instruction 1)\n- **Provisional Quantities\
      \ and Payment for Measured Work** (Instruction 2)\n- **Price Bid Preparation in the Prescribed XLS Sheet**\
      \ (Instruction 3; ITT 10)\n  - Schedule-A: percentage rate to two decimals; At Par / Excess / Less\n  - Schedule-B:\
      \ At Par only\n  - Schedule-C: lump-sum salvage credit to BMRCL\n- **Proportionate Recovery and Adjustment\
      \ of Salvage Credit** (Instruction 4)\n- **Salvage Credit Based on Quoted Price** (Instruction 5)\n- **Taxes\
      \ and Statutory Levies Included, Except GST** (Instruction 6)\n- **Measurement of Completed Work** (Instruction\
      \ 7)\n- **Completed-Work Pricing and Included Execution Costs** (Instruction 8)\n- **Included Safety and Protection\
      \ Costs** (Instruction 9)\n- **Overheads, Profit, Risks and Defect-Remedy Obligations** (Instruction 10)\n\
      - **Accommodation, Insurance, Wages and Approval Costs** (Instruction 11)\n\n\n\n- **Execution Standards and\
      \ Engineer’s Directions** (Instruction 12)\n- **Incidental Removal Works Without Separate Payment** (Instruction\
      \ 13)\n- **Temporary Relocation and Protection of Utilities** (Instruction 14)\n- **Temporary Works, Power\
      \ and Supports at Contractor’s Cost** (Instruction 15)\n- **Permanent Supports and Restoration Payment Provisions**\
      \ (Instruction 16)\n- **Balcony and Canopy Area at 50%; Chejjas Excluded from Payment** (Instruction 17)\n\
      - **Demolition Depth and Refilling of Low-Level Areas** (Instruction 18)\n- **Removal Within the Line of Cut,\
      \ Excluding Trees** (Instruction 19)\n- **Total Floor Area as the Sum of Built Areas of All Floors** (Instruction\
      \ 20)\n- **Building Additions or Deletions and Accepted Percentage Rates** (Instruction 21)"
    page_basis: OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified
    page_precision: section_reference

        quote: "{\n  \"page_type\": \"text\",\n  \"pdf_page\": 6,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
          ,\n  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                   \
          \     Financial Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                        \
          \    September 2026 \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill\
          \ of Quantities (BOQ) & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the\
          \ entire Tender and Contract Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract\
          \ (including ‘Contract Data’), \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications,\
          \ \\nEmployer’s Requirements, Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum\
          \ and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated\
          \ \\n(provisional). The basis of payment will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried\
          \ out, as measured by the Contractor and verified by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted\
          \ priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”. \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls\
          \ format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage\
          \ rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter\
          \ percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A, for Schedule-B tenderer is required to\
          \ \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL\
          \ on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount indicated for Schedule C will\
          \ be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of any variation in Quantities\
          \ (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according to the value\
          \ of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor should note that\
          \ the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price and not on the\
          \ estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes,\
          \ duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month\
          \ previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The\
          \ method of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements\
          \ as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities\
          \ or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of\
          \ work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed\
          \ to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power,\
          \ water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility\
          \ (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts, all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing,\
          \ watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will\
          \ be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for\
          \ workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining land & building while working.\
          \ \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead charges, \\ncontractor’s\
          \ profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities \\nand obligations set\
          \ out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the Defect Liability Period.\
          \  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff & workmen, \\nall\
          \ insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\ncharges payable\
          \ to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n\

          \ to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n\
          \    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        14.760004043579102,\n        37.509437561035156,\n        519.9669799804688,\n\
          \        56.52032470703125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 0,\n      \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157\
          \                                        Financial Document \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\":\
          \ [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        62.5140495300293,\n        74.49351501464844,\n        69.48028564453125\n\
          \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 1,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n  \
          \      71.99996948242188,\n        777.8273315429688,\n        74.38514709472656,\n        786.9194946289062\n\
          \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 2,\n      \"text\": \" \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n  \
          \      71.99996948242188,\n        802.54736328125,\n        525.8132934570312,\n        823.079833984375\n\
          \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.       \
          \                     September 2026 \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\":\
          \ [\n        227.51991271972656,\n        74.64318084716797,\n        377.9470520019531,\n        84.99869537353516\n\
          \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"Pricing Document-Preamble \\n\"\n    },\n   \
          \ {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99996948242188,\n        99.36321258544922,\n        526.3881225585938,\n\
          \        177.9987030029297\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 5,\n      \"text\": \"1. The Pricing Document-Bill\
          \ of Quantities (BOQ) & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the\
          \ entire Tender and Contract Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract\
          \ (including ‘Contract Data’), \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications,\
          \ \\nEmployer’s Requirements, Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum\
          \ and Addendums. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.999267578125,\n        187.3231201171875,\n\
          \        526.3868408203125,\n        238.59861755371094\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 6,\n      \"\
          text\": \"2. The quantities given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional).\
          \ The basis of payment will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried out, as measured by\
          \ the Contractor and verified by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price\
          \ Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99929809570312,\n\
          \        247.923095703125,\n        526.387451171875,\n        326.4385681152344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
          : 7,\n      \"text\": \"3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance\
          \ with the ITT \\nclause 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\\
          nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures\
          \ on estimated cost to Schedule-A, for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option\
          \ as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials,\
          \ in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99847412109375,\n        335.7630310058594,\n\
          \        526.3863525390625,\n        387.0384826660156\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"\
          text\": \"4. The Lump sum amount indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount\
          \ bills proportionately. In case of any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit\
          \ to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills\
          \ on pro-rata basis. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99948120117188,\n        396.36297607421875,\n\
          \        526.3870239257812,\n        420.3984680175781\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"\
          text\": \"5. The Contractor should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\\
          nreference to the quoted price and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n    \
          \  \"bbox\": [\n        71.99951171875,\n        429.72296142578125,\n        526.3870239257812,\n   \
          \     467.31842041015625\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 10,\n      \"text\": \"6. The ‘Tender Price’\
          \ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as\
          \ per the laws in force as on last day of the month previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender,\
          \ except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00054931640625,\n\
          \        476.6429138183594,\n        526.3883666992188,\n        514.2384033203125\n      ],\n      \"\
          block_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"7. The method of measurement of completed work for payment shall\
          \ be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in\
          \ the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n\
          \      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00054931640625,\n        523.5628662109375,\n        526.3883666992188,\n\
          \        602.1983642578125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"8. The quoted price\
          \ is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions\
          \ of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel,\
          \ oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified\
          \ C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts, all temporary\
          \ works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection facilities.\
          \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00051879882812,\n        611.5228271484375,\n  \
          \      526.3880615234375,\n        649.1182861328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 13,\n      \"\
          text\": \"9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/ \\\
          ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining\
          \ land & building while working. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00051879882812,\n\
          \        658.4427490234375,\n        526.3875732421875,\n        709.71826171875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
          : 14,\n      \"text\": \"10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead\
          \ charges, \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities\
          \ \\nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring\
          \ the Defect Liability Period.  \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99981689453125,\n\
          \        719.042724609375,\n        526.3865966796875,\n        756.6382446289062\n      ],\n      \"\
          block_number\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations\
          \ for staff & workmen, \\nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries,\
          \ fees if any, \\ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\\
          n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        570.0199584960938,\n\
          \        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 16,\n      \"text\": \"305\\n\"\n    }\n\
          \  ]\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"page_type\": \"text\",\n  \"pdf_page\": 6,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n\
      \  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial\
      \ Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026\
      \ \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill of Quantities (BOQ)\
      \ & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the entire Tender and Contract\
      \ Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract (including ‘Contract Data’),\
      \ \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications, \\nEmployer’s Requirements,\
      \ Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities\
      \ given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional). The basis of payment\
      \ will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried out, as measured by the Contractor and verified\
      \ by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”.\
      \ \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause\
      \ 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to\
      \ select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A,\
      \ for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer\
      \ shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount\
      \ indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of\
      \ any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased\
      \ according to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor\
      \ should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price\
      \ and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable\
      \ taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month\
      \ previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The method\
      \ of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in\
      \ the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the\
      \ tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all\
      \ respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional\
      \ plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris\
      \ at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts,\
      \ all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection\
      \ facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/\
      \ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining\
      \ land & building while working. \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead\
      \ charges, \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities\
      \ \\nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the\
      \ Defect Liability Period.  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff\
      \ & workmen, \\nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\\
      ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\

      \ \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99929809570312,\n        247.923095703125,\n       \
      \ 526.387451171875,\n        326.4385681152344\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 7,\n      \"text\": \"3.\
      \ Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause 10.\
      \ Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to select\
      \ option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A,\
      \ for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer\
      \ shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n   \
      \   \"bbox\": [\n        71.99847412109375,\n        335.7630310058594,\n        526.3863525390625,\n    \
      \    387.0384826660156\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"4. The Lump sum amount indicated\
      \ for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of any variation\
      \ in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according\
      \ to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n     \
      \ \"bbox\": [\n        71.99948120117188,\n        396.36297607421875,\n        526.3870239257812,\n     \
      \   420.3984680175781\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"5. The Contractor should note\
      \ that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price and not on the\
      \ estimate cost put to tender. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99951171875,\n        429.72296142578125,\n\
      \        526.3870239257812,\n        467.31842041015625\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 10,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand\
      \ statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month previous to the \\nmonth of invitation\
      \ of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00054931640625,\n\
      \        476.6429138183594,\n        526.3883666992188,\n        514.2384033203125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 11,\n      \"text\": \"7. The method of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance\
      \ with \\nthe requirements as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument-\
      \ Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the tender documents. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n     \
      \   72.00054931640625,\n        523.5628662109375,\n        526.3883666992188,\n        602.1983642578125\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"8. The quoted price is for completed and finished\
      \ items of work and complete in all respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be\
      \ deemed to have included all \\nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power,\
      \ water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished\
      \ material), all leads & lifts, all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance,\
      \ night working and inspection facilities. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00051879882812,\n\
      \        611.5228271484375,\n        526.3880615234375,\n        649.1182861328125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 13,\n      \"text\": \"9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work\
      \ sites/ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection\
      \ of adjoining land & building while working. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00051879882812,\n\
      \        658.4427490234375,\n        526.3875732421875,\n        709.71826171875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 14,\n      \"text\": \"10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead charges,\
      \ \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities \\nand obligations\
      \ set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the Defect Liability Period.\
      \  \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99981689453125,\n        719.042724609375,\n      \
      \  526.3865966796875,\n        756.6382446289062\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"\
      11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff & workmen, \\nall insurance\
      \ costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\ncharges payable to various\
      \ departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        546.0439453125,\n\
      \        24.479991912841797,\n        570.0199584960938,\n        41.38799285888672\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 16,\n      \"text\": \"305\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"
